class="mdx-content @container/columns-container relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="Connect to Supabase" data-page-href="/integrations/supabase" id="content"><span data-as="p">The Supabase integration connects a <a href="https://supabase.com/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Supabase</a> project you own to your Lovable project and uses it as your app’s backend. When connected, Lovable builds full-stack features directly from chat: it designs your database schema, runs migrations, deploys edge functions, and wires your app’s UI to your data, all against your own Supabase project.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><path d="M 9 1.6714 C 13.0371 1.6714 16.3286 4.9629 16.3286 9 C 16.3286 13.0371 13.0371 16.3286 9 16.3286 C 7.0574 16.3252 5.1953 15.552 3.8217 14.1783 C 2.448 12.8047 1.6748 10.9426 1.6714 9 C 1.6714 4.9629 4.9629 1.6714 9 1.6714 Z M 9 0 C 4.0371 0 0 4.0371 0 9 C 0 13.9629 4.0371 18 9 18 C 13.9629 18 18 13.9629 18 9 C 18 4.0371 13.9629 0 9 0 Z M 10.2857 3.8571 H 7.7143 V 10.2857 H 10.2857 V 3.8571 Z M 10.2857 11.5714 H 7.7143 V 14.1429 H 10.2857 V 11.5714 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p"><strong>Do I need Supabase?</strong> No. Lovable’s <a class="link" href="/features/cloud">built-in backend (Cloud)</a> is enabled by default, so most projects never need a separate Supabase account. It utilizes Supabase’s open-source foundation, so you get the same database, auth, storage, and edge function capabilities without any setup.</span><span data-as="p">Connect your own Supabase project when you want direct ownership of the backend: your own Supabase account and billing, full access to the Supabase dashboard, or an existing Supabase project you already use.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The Supabase integration is available on all plans.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="supabase-or-the-built-in-backend"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#supabase-or-the-built-in-backend" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Supabase or the built-in backend?</span></h2>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_14llktbsnlhjiuasnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px] [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums"><thead><tr><th></th><th>Built-in backend (Cloud)</th><th>Your own Supabase project</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Setup</td><td>Automatic, no extra account</td><td>Requires a Supabase account and project</td></tr><tr><td>Billing</td><td>Credits in your Lovable workspace</td><td>Your Supabase subscription, billed by Supabase</td></tr><tr><td>Backend management</td><td>The Cloud view inside Lovable</td><td>Supabase dashboard</td></tr><tr><td>Auth settings and social login providers</td><td>Configured through Lovable</td><td>Configured in the Supabase dashboard</td></tr><tr><td>Infrastructure (backups, instance size, pausing)</td><td>Managed by Lovable</td><td>Managed by you in Supabase</td></tr><tr><td>Best for</td><td>Most projects, fastest path to a working backend</td><td>Teams who want to own their Supabase account and infrastructure</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Switching between the two is not automatic. There is no one-click migration from the built-in backend (Cloud) to Supabase or the other way, so it’s worth deciding early which backend you want a project to use. See <a class="link" href="/features/advanced-settings#export-lovable-cloud-data">Export Lovable Cloud data</a> for details on exporting Cloud data.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="what-you-can-build"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#what-you-can-build" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">What you can build</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">With Supabase connected, you can prompt Lovable to build complete backend features:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Store and query data</strong>: Lovable designs tables and relationships from a plain-language description and connects your UI to them.</li>
<li><strong>Sign users in</strong>: email and password authentication, social logins, and access control backed by Supabase Auth.</li>
<li><strong>Upload and serve files</strong>: images, documents, and other media stored in Supabase Storage buckets.</li>
<li><strong>Run server-side code</strong>: Supabase Edge Functions for payments, emails, AI features, scheduled tasks, and external API calls.</li>
<li><strong>Keep secrets safe</strong>: API keys for services like Stripe or Resend are stored securely in your Supabase project, never in your app’s code.</li>
<li><strong>Update in real time</strong>: live chat, feeds, and dashboards using Supabase’s real-time subscriptions.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">For the full picture of everything the Supabase platform offers, see the <a href="https://supabase.com/docs" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Supabase documentation</a>.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="prerequisites"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#prerequisites" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Prerequisites</span></h2>
<ul>
<li>A Supabase account. <a href="https://supabase.com/dashboard/sign-up" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Sign up</a> or <a href="https://supabase.com/dashboard/sign-in" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">sign in</a>; the free tier is enough to get started.</li>
<li>A Supabase project to connect, or a Supabase organization where you can create one.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="connect-supabase-to-lovable"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#connect-supabase-to-lovable" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Connect Supabase to Lovable</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Connecting involves two separate actions, often done by different people:</span>
<ol>
<li><strong>Link a Supabase organization to your Lovable workspace.</strong> A workspace owner or admin does this once per organization. Linked organizations become available to everyone in the workspace.</li>
<li><strong>Connect a Lovable project to a Supabase project.</strong> Anyone with edit access to the project can do this, using any project from a linked organization.</li>
</ol>
<span data-as="p">If you’re a workspace owner or admin doing this for the first time, the connect dialog walks you through both actions in one flow.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="link-your-supabase-organization-workspace-owners-and-admins"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#link-your-supabase-organization-workspace-owners-and-admins" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Link your Supabase organization (workspace owners and admins)</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">You can link an organization from any of these places:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>The Supabase connector page</strong>: open <a class="link" href="/integrations/introduction"><strong>Connectors</strong></a> from the dashboard sidebar, select <strong>Supabase</strong>, and click <strong>Connect Supabase</strong> (or <strong>Manage Connected Organizations</strong> if the workspace already has one).</li>
<li><strong>The connect dialog in a project</strong>: in the editor, open <strong>More → Cloud</strong>, click <strong>Already have a Supabase project? Connect it here</strong>, and use <strong>Add Organization</strong>.</li>
<li><strong>When creating a project</strong>: on the dashboard, click <strong>+</strong> in the prompt box, open <strong>Databases</strong>, and choose <strong>Add Supabase organization</strong>.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Whichever you choose, a Supabase window opens where you sign in and authorize Lovable for the organization you pick. The organization then appears under <strong>Linked Supabase organizations</strong> and is available to every member of the workspace. Members don’t need their own Supabase accounts to connect projects from it.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><path d="M 9 1.6714 C 13.0371 1.6714 16.3286 4.9629 16.3286 9 C 16.3286 13.0371 13.0371 16.3286 9 16.3286 C 7.0574 16.3252 5.1953 15.552 3.8217 14.1783 C 2.448 12.8047 1.6748 10.9426 1.6714 9 C 1.6714 4.9629 4.9629 1.6714 9 1.6714 Z M 9 0 C 4.0371 0 0 4.0371 0 9 C 0 13.9629 4.0371 18 9 18 C 13.9629 18 18 13.9629 18 9 C 18 4.0371 13.9629 0 9 0 Z M 10.2857 3.8571 H 7.7143 V 10.2857 H 10.2857 V 3.8571 Z M 10.2857 11.5714 H 7.7143 V 14.1429 H 10.2857 V 11.5714 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Only workspace owners and admins can add or reconnect organizations. If these options are disabled for you, ask a workspace admin to link the organization first.</span></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="connect-your-lovable-project-to-a-supabase-project"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#connect-your-lovable-project-to-a-supabase-project" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Connect your Lovable project to a Supabase project</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">When an organization is linked, anyone with edit access to a project can connect it. Start from either place:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>When creating a new project</strong>: on the dashboard, click <strong>+</strong> in the prompt box, open <strong>Databases</strong>, and pick a Supabase project under the <strong>Supabase</strong> heading (or type “Supabase” in the menu search). The new Lovable project is created with that Supabase project as its backend.</li>
<li><strong>In an existing project</strong>: connect from the Cloud view in the editor:</li>
</ul>
<div role="list" class="steps ml-3.5 mt-10 mb-6"><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="group-has-[a:focus-visible]/step-indicator:opacity-0">1</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Open the connect dialog</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">In the editor, open <strong>More → Cloud</strong> and click <strong>Already have a Supabase project? Connect it here</strong>. The link appears on projects that don’t have a backend yet, next to the <strong>Enable Cloud</strong> option.</span><span data-as="p">In the <strong>Linked Supabase organizations</strong> dialog, click <strong>Continue</strong>.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="group-has-[a:focus-visible]/step-indicator:opacity-0">2</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Choose a Supabase project</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">The <strong>Connect project</strong> step lists the Supabase projects in your linked organizations. Click <strong>Connect</strong> next to the project you want to use.</span><span data-as="p">Don’t have a Supabase project yet? Click <strong>Create Project</strong> to open the Supabase dashboard, create one there, and then return to Lovable to connect it.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-transparent bg-linear-to-b from-gray-200 dark:from-white/10 via-80% to-transparent group-has-[[data-component-part=&quot;step-content&quot;]:empty]/step:hidden" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="group-has-[a:focus-visible]/step-indicator:opacity-0">3</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Verify the connection</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">After connecting, the Cloud view shows your Supabase project’s name and status, with quick links to the Supabase dashboard. You can now prompt Lovable to build backend features.</span></div></div></div></div>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><path d="M 9 1.6714 C 13.0371 1.6714 16.3286 4.9629 16.3286 9 C 16.3286 13.0371 13.0371 16.3286 9 16.3286 C 7.0574 16.3252 5.1953 15.552 3.8217 14.1783 C 2.448 12.8047 1.6748 10.9426 1.6714 9 C 1.6714 4.9629 4.9629 1.6714 9 1.6714 Z M 9 0 C 4.0371 0 0 4.0371 0 9 C 0 13.9629 4.0371 18 9 18 C 13.9629 18 18 13.9629 18 9 C 18 4.0371 13.9629 0 9 0 Z M 10.2857 3.8571 H 7.7143 V 10.2857 H 10.2857 V 3.8571 Z M 10.2857 11.5714 H 7.7143 V 14.1429 H 10.2857 V 11.5714 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">If the connect link shows <strong>Wait for Lovable to finish working</strong>, Lovable is still processing a request in this project. Wait for it to finish, then try again.</span></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="how-lovable-works-with-your-supabase-project"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#how-lovable-works-with-your-supabase-project" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">How Lovable works with your Supabase project</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">With Supabase connected, Lovable manages your backend from chat. You don’t need to write SQL or copy code into the Supabase dashboard.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="database"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#database" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Database</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Supabase provides a hosted PostgreSQL database, so your app keeps the full power and scalability of SQL without you managing servers. Whenever a feature needs to store data, describe it in plain language and Lovable designs the tables, columns, and relationships for you:</span>

users can leave a message and a 1 to 5 rating, and store the responses in the database.</span></span>

<span data-as="p">Schema changes run as reviewed migrations: Lovable writes the SQL, shows it to you, and asks for your approval in chat before running it. After you approve, Lovable executes the migration on your Supabase project, saves the migration file in your project’s code (under <code>supabase/migrations/</code>), and regenerates the TypeScript types your app uses. Lovable also asks for your approval before inserting or changing data. Reading data for debugging (for example, checking why a page is empty) does not modify anything.</span>
<span data-as="p">You can always work with your data directly in the Supabase dashboard as well: the <strong>Table Editor</strong> shows rows in a spreadsheet-like view where you can inspect and edit records, and the <strong>SQL Editor</strong> lets you run your own queries.</span>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Real-time updates</strong>: Supabase can stream database changes to your app, which enables features like live chat, notifications, or dashboards that update for all users without refreshing. Prompt Lovable to make a feature update in real time and it wires up the subscription:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span data-as="p">Lovable wires the pages to your Supabase project, so users who register through your app appear under <strong>Authentication</strong> in the Supabase dashboard, where you can also add test users manually.</span>
<span data-as="p">Authentication settings themselves live in your Supabase project, not in Lovable. To offer social logins (such as Google or GitHub), enable the provider in your Supabase dashboard’s <strong>Authentication</strong> settings and supply the OAuth credentials the provider requires. Then prompt Lovable to add the matching sign-in button and flow to your app:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 Re-enable it before launching to real users.</span></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="edge-functions"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#edge-functions" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Edge functions</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Supabase Edge Functions are serverless functions: small pieces of backend code that run on Supabase’s infrastructure, without you provisioning or managing a server. They’re the right tool whenever your app needs logic that can’t or shouldn’t run in the user’s browser. Typical uses:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>AI features</strong>: calling a model API to summarize, classify, or chat.</li>
<li><strong>Sending emails or notifications</strong>: for example, a welcome email on signup via an email API like Resend.</li>
<li><strong>Processing payments</strong>: creating checkout sessions and handling webhooks with Stripe.</li>
<li><strong>Scheduled tasks</strong>: recurring jobs like a nightly cleanup or a daily summary.</li>
<li><strong>Calling external APIs with secret keys</strong>: anything that uses credentials you can’t expose in frontend code.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">You don’t write these functions yourself. Describe the behavior in chat, and Lovable writes the function, deploys it to your Supabase project, and updates your app to call it:</span>

<span data-as="p">When a function fails, Lovable reads its logs and surfaces the error in chat to help you fix it. You can also monitor your functions yourself in the Supabase dashboard under <strong>Edge Functions</strong>, where each function has its own logs.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="secrets"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#secrets" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Secrets</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Many backend features depend on secret API keys, such as a Stripe secret key for payments or a Resend key for email. When a feature needs one, Lovable detects it and prompts you to enter the value through a secure input. Secrets are stored in your Supabase project, where your edge functions can read them; they never appear in your app’s code or repository, and Lovable does not read stored secret values back.</span>

<span class="line"><span></span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">For example, when Lovable deploys a payment function, the function reads your Stripe secret key from Supabase’s secret store to authenticate with Stripe. You can manage stored secrets in the Supabase dashboard under your project’s <strong>Edge Functions</strong> settings.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="storage"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#storage" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Storage</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Supabase Storage hosts files (images, videos, PDFs, and other media) alongside your database, organized into buckets with their own access permissions. Ask for a file upload feature and Lovable builds the upload logic and creates the buckets, asking for your approval before creating or changing buckets on your Supabase project:</span>

picture on the account settings page and show it in the header.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span></span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Uploaded files are stored in a bucket in your Supabase project, and Lovable handles retrieving the file URLs to display them in your app. You can browse files and manage bucket permissions in the Supabase dashboard under <strong>Storage</strong>. Upload size limits follow your Supabase plan (on Supabase’s free tier, individual files are limited to 50 MB); see <a href="https://supabase.com/pricing" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Supabase pricing</a> for current limits.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="security-checks"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#security-checks" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Security checks</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Lovable runs automated security checks against your database configuration, including Row Level Security (RLS) coverage, and flags issues it finds after schema changes.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Warning" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="warning"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="flex-none size-5 text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300"><path d="M 9 6.75 v 1.5 m 0 3 h 0.0075 m -5.2035 3 h 10.392 c 1.155 0 1.8765 -1.2503 1.299 -2.25 L 10.299 3 c -0.5775 -0.9997 -2.0205 -0.9997 -2.598 0 L 2.505 12 c -0.5775 0.9997 0.144 2.25 1.299 2.25 z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p"><strong>Before going live</strong>, make sure every table has Row Level Security policies that restrict who can read and write each row. Ask Lovable to review your policies (for example, “Make sure users can only see and edit their own data”), and verify them yourself in the Supabase dashboard. Missing RLS policies are the most common way app data gets exposed.</span></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="managing-the-connection"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#managing-the-connection" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Managing the connection</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The connection has two levels, managed in different places: the <strong>project connection</strong> (which Supabase project a Lovable project uses) and the workspace’s <strong>linked organizations</strong> (which Supabase organizations the whole workspace can use).</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="manage-the-project-connection"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#manage-the-project-connection" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Manage the project connection</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">In the editor, open <strong>More → Cloud</strong> on a connected project. From there you can:</span>
<ul>
<li>See the connected project’s name and health status.</li>
<li>Jump to the Supabase dashboard: the project overview, user management, SQL editor, edge functions, and secrets.</li>
<li>Disconnect the project.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Your backend lives in Supabase, so day-to-day management of data, users, and files happens in the Supabase dashboard.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="manage-linked-organizations-workspace-owners-and-admins"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#manage-linked-organizations-workspace-owners-and-admins" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Manage linked organizations (workspace owners and admins)</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Open <a class="link" href="/integrations/introduction"><strong>Connectors</strong></a> from the dashboard sidebar, select <strong>Supabase</strong>, and use <strong>Manage Connected Organizations</strong> to link additional Supabase organizations or reconnect one whose access has expired. The <strong>Manage organizations</strong> button in a connected project’s Cloud view opens the same place.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="disconnect-supabase"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#disconnect-supabase" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Disconnect Supabase</span></h3>
<div role="list" class="steps ml-3.5 mt-10 mb-6"><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="group-has-[a:focus-visible]/step-indicator:opacity-0">1</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Open the Cloud view</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">In the editor, open <strong>More → Cloud</strong>. The view shows your connected Supabase project.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-transparent bg-linear-to-b from-gray-200 dark:from-white/10 via-80% to-transparent group-has-[[data-component-part=&quot;step-content&quot;]:empty]/step:hidden" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="group-has-[a:focus-visible]/step-indicator:opacity-0">2</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Disconnect</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">Click <strong>Disconnect</strong>, then confirm in the <strong>Disconnect Supabase</strong> dialog.</span></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Disconnecting stops Lovable from deploying edge functions and reading your database schema, but it does not change your code and does not delete anything in your Supabase project. You can reconnect to the same or a different Supabase project later.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="limitations"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#limitations" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Limitations</span></h2>
<ul>
<li>Each Lovable project connects to one Supabase project at a time.</li>
<li>There is no automatic migration between the built-in backend (Cloud) and your own Supabase project, in either direction.</li>
<li>Supabase usage limits (database size, file upload size, bandwidth) follow your Supabase plan. Check <a href="https://supabase.com/pricing" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Supabase pricing</a> for current limits.</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="lovable-features-that-require-the-built-in-backend"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#lovable-features-that-require-the-built-in-backend" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Lovable features that require the built-in backend</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Some Lovable features are built for the <a class="link" href="/features/cloud">built-in backend (Cloud)</a> and don’t apply when you bring your own Supabase project:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>In-editor backend views</strong>: the Database, Users, Storage, Secrets, Jobs, Usage, and Logs views in the Cloud section. With your own Supabase project, you use the Supabase dashboard instead.</li>
<li><strong>Managed infrastructure</strong>: daily backups with restore, pausing, and instance resizing from inside Lovable. You manage backups, compute, and billing in Supabase.</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/features/payments"><strong>Built-in payments</strong></a>: Paddle and Stripe payments through Lovable require the built-in backend for webhooks and subscription data.</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/features/custom-emails"><strong>Custom emails</strong></a>: sending your app’s emails from your own domain requires the built-in backend.</li>
<li><strong>Configuring auth through Lovable</strong>: enabling social login providers and changing auth settings from chat, including <a class="link" href="/features/google-auth">managed Google authentication</a> and <a class="link" href="/features/saml-sso">SAML SSO for your app’s users</a>, works on the built-in backend only. On your own Supabase project, configure these in the Supabase dashboard.</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/features/browser-testing"><strong>Signed-in browser testing</strong></a>: Lovable can test your app as a logged-in user on the built-in backend only; with your own Supabase project it tests pages that don’t require signing in.</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/features/sensitive-data-scanning"><strong>Sensitive data scanning</strong></a>: PII detection covers built-in databases and storage.</li>
</ul>

Connect your own Supabase project only if you want to own and manage the backend yourself.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="do-i-need-a-separate-supabase-account-and-who-bills-me-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="do-i-need-a-separate-supabase-account-and-who-bills-me" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="do-i-need-a-separate-supabase-account-and-who-bills-me-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Do I need a separate Supabase account, and who bills me?</p></div></summary><div id="do-i-need-a-separate-supabase-account-and-who-bills-me-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="do-i-need-a-separate-supabase-account-and-who-bills-me-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">Yes. Supabase is a separate platform with its own account and billing. Lovable credits cover the building work Lovable does; everything your backend consumes (database, storage, bandwidth) is billed by Supabase under your Supabase plan. Both platforms have free tiers, so you can start without paying.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="who-in-my-workspace-can-connect-supabase-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="who-in-my-workspace-can-connect-supabase" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="who-in-my-workspace-can-connect-supabase-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Who in my workspace can connect Supabase?</p></div></summary><div id="who-in-my-workspace-can-connect-supabase-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="who-in-my-workspace-can-connect-supabase-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">Workspace owners and admins link Supabase organizations to the workspace. After an organization is linked, anyone with edit access to a project can connect it to a Supabase project from that organization, without needing their own Supabase account. Viewers can’t connect projects.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="do-i-still-need-to-paste-sql-into-the-supabase-sql-editor-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="do-i-still-need-to-paste-sql-into-the-supabase-sql-editor" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="do-i-still-need-to-paste-sql-into-the-supabase-sql-editor-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Do I still need to paste SQL into the Supabase SQL editor?</p></div></summary><div id="do-i-still-need-to-paste-sql-into-the-supabase-sql-editor-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="do-i-still-need-to-paste-sql-into-the-supabase-sql-editor-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">No. This was how the integration used to work, but Lovable now runs database migrations for you. It shows you the change, asks for approval in chat, executes the migration on your Supabase project, and keeps migration files and generated types in your project’s code.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="can-i-switch-a-project-between-the-built-in-backend-and-my-own-supabase-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="can-i-switch-a-project-between-the-built-in-backend-and-my-own-supabase" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="can-i-switch-a-project-between-the-built-in-backend-and-my-own-supabase-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Can I switch a project between the built-in backend and my own Supabase?</p></div></summary><div id="can-i-switch-a-project-between-the-built-in-backend-and-my-own-supabase-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="can-i-switch-a-project-between-the-built-in-backend-and-my-own-supabase-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">There is no automatic migration in either direction. If you need to move, you’ll recreate the schema and move data manually: export your data from the current backend, connect the new one, and ask Lovable to rebuild the schema. For exporting your data, see <a class="link" href="/features/advanced-settings#export-lovable-cloud-data">Export Lovable Cloud data</a>.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="can-multiple-lovable-projects-share-one-supabase-project-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="can-multiple-lovable-projects-share-one-supabase-project" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="can-multiple-lovable-projects-share-one-supabase-project-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Can multiple Lovable projects share one Supabase project?</p></div></summary><div id="can-multiple-lovable-projects-share-one-supabase-project-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="can-multiple-lovable-projects-share-one-supabase-project-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">Yes. Each Lovable project connects to one Supabase project, but nothing stops several Lovable projects from connecting to the same Supabase project (for example, a customer app and an admin dashboard sharing one database). All of them read and write the same data, so design your schema and RLS policies with that in mind.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="can-i-connect-my-app-to-automation-tools-like-zapier-or-make-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="can-i-connect-my-app-to-automation-tools-like-zapier-or-make" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="can-i-connect-my-app-to-automation-tools-like-zapier-or-make-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Can I connect my app to automation tools like Zapier or Make?</p></div></summary><div id="can-i-connect-my-app-to-automation-tools-like-zapier-or-make-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="can-i-connect-my-app-to-automation-tools-like-zapier-or-make-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">Yes. Ask Lovable to create an edge function that acts as a custom API endpoint or webhook for the external service; this works the same way on the built-in backend. With your own Supabase project, you additionally get direct access to Supabase’s auto-generated REST API and client libraries, so external tools can also read and write your data without going through an endpoint you build.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="what-happens-when-i-disconnect-supabase-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="what-happens-when-i-disconnect-supabase" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="what-happens-when-i-disconnect-supabase-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">What happens when I disconnect Supabase?</p></div></summary><div id="what-happens-when-i-disconnect-supabase-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="what-happens-when-i-disconnect-supabase-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">Lovable stops deploying edge functions and reading your database schema. Your code is not changed, and nothing is deleted from your Supabase project. You can reconnect later.</span></div></details></div></div><div class="my-8 flex flex-col"><p class="py-1.5 text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 tracking-[-0.1px]">Related topics</p><a class="flex _jsxs(_Fragment,{children:[_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`The Supabase integration connects a `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://supabase.com/`,children:`Supabase`}),` project you own to your Lovable project and uses it as your app’s backend. When connected, Lovable builds full-stack features directly from chat: it designs your database schema, runs migrations, deploys edge functions, and wires your app’s UI to your data, all against your own Supabase project.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Note,{children:[_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Do I need Supabase?`}),` No. Lovable’s `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/features/cloud`,children:`built-in backend (Cloud)`}),` is enabled by default, so most projects never need a separate Supabase account. It utilizes Supabase’s open-source foundation, so you get the same database, auth, storage, and edge function capabilities without any setup.`]}),_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Connect your own Supabase project when you want direct ownership of the backend: your own Supabase account and billing, full access to the Supabase dashboard, or an existing Supabase project you already use.`})]}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`The Supabase integration is available on all plans.`}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`supabase-or-the-built-in-backend`,children:`Supabase or the data.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`what-you-can-build`,children:`What you can build`}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`With Supabase connected, you can prompt Lovable to build complete from a plain-language description and connects your UI to them.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Sign users in`}),`: email and password authentication, social logins, and access control backed by Supabase Auth.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Upload and serve files`}),`: images, documents, and other media stored in Supabase Storage buckets.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Run server-side code`}),`: Supabase Edge Functions for payments, emails, AI features,
